Not yet published on mainnet. Its interface may change before publication.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidTradeFeeBps` | A trade fee is above the launch configuration's ceiling. |
| 1 | `EDecimalsNotAllowed` | The Meme decimals are not admitted for new launches. |
| 2 | `EVenueNotAllowed` | The venue is not admitted for new launches. |
| 3 | `EInvalidVenue` | A primitive or framework type cannot be a venue. |
| 4 | `EInvalidProtocolAllocation` | Blast's allocation exceeds its 1,000 bps maximum for either asset. |
| 5 | `EInvalidShares` | The shares do not sum to 10,000, or the curve or pool Meme share is zero. |
| 6 | `ERecipientCapacityReached` | A split has more than sixteen recipients. |
| 7 | `EInvalidSellBurnBps` | The sell-burn rate is above its ceiling. |
| 8 | `EInvalidInitialMemeSupply` | The ticket's supply is not the canonical one billion tokens. |
| 9 | `EInvalidMemeQuotePair` | Meme and Quote are the same type. |
| 10 | `EInvalidOpeningRound` | The opening round is neither zero nor within its bounds. |
| 11 | `EInvalidGraduationTarget` | The pool's Quote share of the graduation target rounds to zero. |
| 12 | `EInvalidLaunchLifecycle` | The launch is not in the state this operation needs. |
| 13 | `ESlippageExceeded` | The output is below the caller's minimum. |
| 14 | `EZeroTradeOutput` | The trade's net output is zero. |
| 15 | `EOpeningRoundEnded` | The opening round has ended. |
| 16 | `EOpeningRoundNotEnded` | The opening round has not ended, or the launch is not shared yet. |
| 17 | `ENothingAccrued` | There are no fees to claim. |
| 18 | `ECreatorCapNotLive` | The creator cap is not the one the launch honors. |
| 19 | `EUnchanged` | A setter was called with the value already in place. |
| 20 | `EInvalidCreatorRecipient` | The creator recipient address is zero. |
| 21 | `EInvalidAccountCommitment` | The account commitment is not 32 bytes. |
| 22 | `EInvalidCreatorClaimPublicKey` | The claim signer key is neither empty nor 32 bytes. |
| 23 | `EInvalidCreatorClaimAttestation` | The claim attestation is unexpected or malformed. |
| 24 | `ECreatorClaimExpired` | The claim attestation has expired. |
| 25 | `ECreatorNotSender` | The sender is not the creator recipient. |
| 26 | `EInvalidCreatorClaimSignature` | The claim attestation's signature does not verify. |
| 27 | `EQuoteNotAllowed` | The Quote is not admitted for new launches. |
| 28 | `EInvalidVirtualQuote` | The virtual Quote reserve is zero, which would let the first purchase take the whole curve. |
| 29 | `EZeroOpeningRoundBid` | An opening-round bid is zero. |
| 30 | `EInvalidCreationFee` | The creation fee paid is not exactly the configured one, or a configured fee exceeds 10 SUI. |
| 31 | `ETooManyClaimSigners` | The account-claim signer set already holds its most live and pending keys. |
| 32 | `EUnguardedDelay` | Unpausing needs every policy delay nonzero, with no pending zero delay or admission. |
| 33 | `EInvalidProtocolFeeShare` | Blast's share of the trade fee exceeds its 7,000 bps maximum. |
| 34 | `EInvalidLpFeeBps` | Blast's LP-fee share exceeds its 3,000 bps maximum for either asset. |

### `blast_fun::allocation`

Migration allocation plans and their exact apportionment.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/math/allocation.move

#### Types

```move
/// One recipient at migration, paid outright or vested, with its shares of the Meme supply and
/// the final Quote reserve.
public struct RecipientShare has copy, drop, store {
    recipient: blast_core::payout::Recipient,
    meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
}

/// How migration divides the Meme supply and the final Quote reserve between the curve, the pool,
/// and the recipients. Each asset sums to 10,000 once Blast's allocation is added.
public struct AllocationPlan has copy, drop, store {
    curve_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    protocol_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    protocol_quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    recipients: vector<RecipientShare>,
}

/// Blast's one-time Meme and Quote shares, accrued as protocol fees at migration.
public struct ProtocolAllocation has copy, drop, store {
    meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// A plan share: `recipient` (`blast_core::payout::recipient`, which rejects the zero address)
/// receives `meme_bps` of the Meme supply and `quote_bps` of the final Quote reserve at migration.
public fun recipient_share(
    recipient: blast_core::payout::Recipient,
    meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
): RecipientShare

/// The curve needs positive Meme and the pool needs positive Meme and Quote shares.
/// Blast's allocation is added at finalization.
public fun plan(
    curve_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_meme_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    lp_quote_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    recipients: vector<RecipientShare>,
): AllocationPlan
```

### `blast_fun::blast_fun`

The launchpad's publication and its package witness.  Publication hands the deployer an owned `Publication<BLAST_FUN>`, which `blast_admin::package_admin::wrap` turns into the shared `PackageAdmin<BLAST_FUN>` and the shared `PackageVersion<BLAST_FUN>`, fully paused. Pausing, upgrades, and immutability go through `blast_admin::package_admin` under the package-scoped roles `PauseAdmin<BLAST_FUN>`, `UpgradeAdmin<BLAST_FUN>`, and `CancelAdmin<BLAST_FUN>`.  The publication pins `PackageWitness`, the one witness the package passes wherever a sealed call must name the launchpad: the launch fee ledger, migration hand-off, vault terms and owner proofs, and the generic administration's pause release. `launch_config::unpause` releases pause bits once every launchpad delay is guarded; it lives beside the configurations it reads, which name `BLAST_FUN`, so this module cannot import them.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/blast_fun.move

#### Types

```move
/// The package's one-time witness (OTW), typing its version, publication, and roles.
public struct BLAST_FUN() has drop;

/// The launchpad's package witness. Only this package constructs it, through `package_witness`;
/// every caller passes it straight into the sealing or release call, and no function stores it or
/// returns it outside this package.
public struct PackageWitness() has drop;
```

### `blast_fun::constants`

Package-wide protocol constants exposed as zero-cost macros.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/lib/constants.move

#### Constants

- `version: u64 = 1`: The package version this bytecode serves.
- `create_pause_bit: u8 = 1`: Stops launch creation, finalization, and sharing.
- `trading_pause_bit: u8 = 2`: Stops buying, selling, and opening-round entry and settlement.
- `migration_pause_bit: u8 = 4`: Stops voluntary launch migration.
- `valid_pause_bits: u8 = blast_fun::constants::create_pause_bit!() | blast_fun::constants::trading_pause_bit!() | blast_fun::constants::migration_pause_bit!()`: Every supported launchpad pause flag.
- `max_recipients: u64 = 16`: Maximum recipients in an allocation plan.
- `max_trade_fee_bps: u64 = 1_000`: Maximum LaunchConfig trade-fee rate for either the meme or quote asset.
- `max_protocol_allocation_bps: u64 = 1_000`: Maximum Blast allocation of a launch's Meme supply or final Quote reserve, each asset.
- `max_protocol_fee_share_bps: u64 = 7_000`: Maximum Blast share of each launch trade fee; the creator receives the rest.
- `max_creation_fee_mist: u64 = 10_000_000_000`: Maximum creation fee, in MIST (10 SUI).
- `max_sell_burn_bps: u64 = 5_000`: Maximum creator-selected share of post-fee Meme sell input reserved for burn.
- `min_opening_round_ms: u64 = 60_000`: Shortest opening round a creator may choose.
- `max_opening_round_ms: u64 = 300_000`: Longest opening round a creator may choose.
- `meme_total_supply_whole_tokens: u64 = 1_000_000_000`: Exact whole-token supply minted for every generated Meme currency.
- `max_claim_signers: u64 = 4`: Most account-claim signer keys live or pending at once: two live during a rotation, two staged.
- `ed25519_public_key_length: u64 = 32`: Canonical Ed25519 public-key length.
- `account_commitment_length: u64 = 32`: Byte length of an opaque Blast account commitment.

### `blast_fun::creator_authority`

Creator recipients, the one-time claim they authorize, and the revocable caps it issues.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/launch/creator_authority.move

#### Types

```move
/// Who may claim a launch's creator caps: an address, or a Blast account the backend attests.
public enum CreatorRecipient has copy, drop, store {
    Address(address),
    Account(vector<u8>),
}

/// Authority over one launch's curve creator fees; the launch honors only the cap ID it records.
public struct CurveFeeCap<phantom Meme> has key, store {
    id: UID,
}

/// Authority over one coin's metadata; the launch honors only the cap ID it records.
public struct CreatorMetadataCap<phantom Meme> has key, store {
    id: UID,
}

/// The BCS message the Blast backend signs for an account claim, after `CREATOR_CLAIM_DOMAIN`.
public struct CreatorClaimMessage has drop {
    meme: TypeName,
    account_commitment: vector<u8>,
    claimant: address,
    expiry_ms: u64,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
public fun new_address(recipient: address): CreatorRecipient

public fun new_account(account_commitment: vector<u8>): CreatorRecipient

/// Deletes a curve-fee cap. Deleting the live cap leaves curve fees unclaimable until an
/// administrator reassigns the cap.
public fun destroy_curve_fee_cap<Meme>(self: CurveFeeCap<Meme>)

/// Deletes a metadata cap. Deleting the live cap freezes the metadata until an administrator
/// reassigns the cap.
public fun destroy_creator_metadata_cap<Meme>(self: CreatorMetadataCap<Meme>)
```

#### Constants

- `CREATOR_CLAIM_DOMAIN: vector<u8> = b"blast_fun::creator_claim::v1"`: Prefixes every signed account-claim message, so the backend key's signature over a claim cannot verify as another message of the same BCS shape.

### `blast_fun::events`

Launchpad event payloads and their package-only emitters. Pauses and versions emit from `blast_admin`, delayed settings from its delayed modules, and Blast's fee payments from `blast_core`.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/lib/events.move

#### Events

```move
public struct MemeDecimalsAllowed has copy, drop {
    decimals: u8,
    allowed: bool,
}

/// A Quote type was admitted to (`allowed = true`) or removed from (`allowed = false`) future
/// launches. Existing launches keep trading in it.
public struct QuoteAllowed<phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    allowed: bool,
}

public struct CreatorCapsClaimed<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    curve_fee_cap_id: ID,
    creator_metadata_cap_id: ID,
}

public struct CurveFeeCapReassigned<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    curve_fee_cap_id: ID,
    recipient: address,
}

public struct CreatorMetadataCapReassigned<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    creator_metadata_cap_id: ID,
    recipient: address,
}

public struct MemeMetadataUpdated<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    name: String,
    description: String,
    icon_url: String,
}

/// A generated package created its currency and launch ticket. The supply is one billion whole
/// tokens at `decimals`.
public struct LaunchTicketCreated<phantom Meme> has copy, drop {
    ticket_id: ID,
    decimals: u8,
    metadata_cap_id: ID,
    creator: creator_authority::CreatorRecipient,
    burnable: bool,
    sell_burn_bps: u64,
}

/// A launch was created from `ticket_id` with these settings; a zero `opening_round_ms` has no
/// opening round. The creator paid `creation_fee` SUI, held with Blast's fees on the launch;
/// `partner_id` is the launch's partner for good, if it names one.
public struct LaunchCreated<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    ticket_id: ID,
    venue: TypeName,
    lp_fee_split: blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit,
    virtual_quote: u64,
    graduation_target: u64,
    quote_fee_bps: u64,
    meme_fee_bps: u64,
    allocation_plan: allocation::AllocationPlan,
    opening_round_ms: u64,
    creation_fee: u64,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
}

public struct LaunchShared<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    public_start_ms: u64,
}

/// `quote_amount` joined a launch's opening round from the sender; `bids` is the pot after it.
public struct OpeningRoundJoined<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    quote_amount: u64,
    bids: u64,
}

/// The opening round closed: its `bids` bought `meme_amount` from the curve, in the same
/// transaction's `Bought` when there is one, and left `quote_amount` to refund. Both are delivered
/// pro rata.
public struct OpeningRoundSettled<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    bids: u64,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
}

/// `bidder`'s share of the opening round, for its `bid`, was sent to it.
public struct OpeningRoundDelivered<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    bidder: address,
    bid: u64,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
}

/// A curve purchase; reserves are after the purchase. Purchases before `LaunchShared` are the
/// creator's, made in the transaction that creates the launch; the one beside
/// `OpeningRoundSettled` is the opening round's pot.
public struct Bought<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    quote_in: u64,
    meme_out_gross: u64,
    meme_out_net: u64,
    quote_refund: u64,
    protocol_meme_fee: u64,
    creator_meme_fee: u64,
    protocol_quote_fee: u64,
    creator_quote_fee: u64,
    meme_reserve: u64,
    real_quote_reserve: u64,
}

/// A curve sale; reserves are after the sale.
public struct Sold<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    meme_in_gross: u64,
    pending_burn: u64,
    quote_out_gross: u64,
    protocol_meme_fee: u64,
    creator_meme_fee: u64,
    protocol_quote_fee: u64,
    creator_quote_fee: u64,
    meme_reserve: u64,
    real_quote_reserve: u64,
}

/// One recipient's allocation, paid when migration begins: as coins to `destination`, or into the
/// vesting schedules named here when the recipient vests.
public struct MigrationAllocationPaid<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    destination: address,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
    meme_vesting_id: Option<ID>,
    quote_vesting_id: Option<ID>,
}

/// Migration committed: the adapter received `meme_amount` and `quote_amount` for the pool, and
/// `disposed_meme` was burned (or sent to `@0x0` under a fixed supply).
public struct Migrated<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
    disposed_meme: u64,
    total_supply_after: u64,
}

public struct CreatorFeesClaimed<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
}

/// Blast's one-time migration Meme and Quote fee joined the launch's protocol ledger.
public struct ProtocolFeesAccrued<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    launch_id: ID,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
}
```

### `blast_fun::launch`

Launch construction, the bonding curve, the migration hand-off, and the owner proof.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/launch/launch.move

#### Types

```move
/// Shared singleton every launch's object ID derives from, so a client finds a coin's one launch
/// from the coin type alone.
public struct LaunchRegistry has key {
    id: UID,
}

/// Derives the one launch of `Meme` from the registry.
public struct LaunchKey<phantom Meme>() has copy, drop, store;

public enum Lifecycle has copy, drop, store {
    OpeningRound,
    Live,
    MigrationReady,
    Migrated,
}

/// Dynamic-field name of one bidder's undelivered opening-round Quote.
public struct OpeningRoundBidKey(address) has copy, drop, store;

/// One launch: its curve, its settings, and custody until migration.
///
/// A launch with an opening round starts in `OpeningRound`. Until `opening_round_ms` after share,
/// Quote joins one pot and nothing trades; settling buys from the curve once with the whole pot, and
/// each bidder is delivered its pro-rata share of the Meme bought and of any Quote refunded.
public struct Launch<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has key {
    id: UID,
    lifecycle: Lifecycle,
    public_start_ms: Option<u64>,
    opening_round_ms: u64,
    /// Total Quote of the opening-round bids not yet delivered.
    opening_round_bids: u64,
    /// The pot until settlement, then the Quote it left to refund.
    opening_round_quote: Balance<Quote>,
    opening_round_meme: Balance<Meme>,
    sell_burn_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    virtual_quote: u64,
    graduation_target: u64,
    quote_fee_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    meme_fee_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    /// The venue witness type the launch's liquidity migrates to.
    venue: TypeName,
    lp_fee_split: blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit,
    atomic_supply: u64,
    meme_reserve: Balance<Meme>,
    real_quote_reserve: Balance<Quote>,
    pending_burn: Balance<Meme>,
    allocation_plan: allocation::AllocationPlan,
    allocated_meme: Balance<Meme>,
    /// Blast's fees: its trade-fee share, the creation fee, and the one-time migration fee.
    protocol_fees: blast_core::protocol_fees::Ledger<Meme, Quote>,
    /// The creator's trade-fee share, claimed with the curve-fee cap.
    creator_meme_fees: Balance<Meme>,
    creator_quote_fees: Balance<Quote>,
    metadata_cap: MetadataCap<Meme>,
    curve_fee_cap_reassignment: blast_admin::delayed_action::DelayedAction<CurveFeeCapReassignment, address>,
    creator_metadata_cap_reassignment: blast_admin::delayed_action::DelayedAction<CreatorMetadataCapReassignment, address>,
    creator: Option<creator_authority::CreatorRecipient>,
    curve_fee_cap_id: Option<ID>,
    creator_metadata_cap_id: Option<ID>,
    /// The Blast partner named at creation, paid its share of every fee Blast takes on this
    /// launch. It never changes.
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
}

/// Keys the curve-fee cap reassignment's delayed lifecycle; its action is the recipient.
public struct CurveFeeCapReassignment() has copy, drop, store;

/// Keys the metadata cap reassignment's delayed lifecycle; its action is the recipient.
public struct CreatorMetadataCapReassignment() has copy, drop, store;

/// Hot potato from `new` to `finalize`, collecting the creator's settings.
public struct LaunchInitializer<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> {
    ticket: launch_ticket::LaunchTicket<Meme>,
    treasury_cap: TreasuryCap<Meme>,
    venue: TypeName,
    quote_fee_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    meme_fee_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    virtual_quote: Option<u64>,
    graduation_target: Option<u64>,
    allocation_plan: Option<allocation::AllocationPlan>,
    lp_fee_split: Option<blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit>,
    opening_round_ms: u64,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Starts a launch whose liquidity migrates to the venue that issued `venue_ticket` after
/// preparing the coin with `treasury_cap`. The Quote, the venue, and the decimals must be admitted.
public fun new<Meme, Quote>(
    ticket: launch_ticket::LaunchTicket<Meme>,
    treasury_cap: TreasuryCap<Meme>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    config: &launch_config::LaunchConfig,
    venue_ticket: blast_core::migration::VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>,
): LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>

/// Sets the virtual Quote reserve the curve starts from, which prices the first purchase.
public fun set_virtual_quote<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    virtual_quote: u64,
)

/// Sets the Quote reserve at which the curve closes.
public fun set_graduation_target<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    graduation_target: u64,
)

public fun set_allocation_plan<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    allocation_plan: allocation::AllocationPlan,
)

public fun set_lp_fee_split<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    lp_fee_split: blast_core::fee_split::FeeSplit,
)

/// Sets how long the opening round runs after share, within the protocol's bounds. Zero, the
/// default, has no opening round: trading starts at share.
public fun set_opening_round<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    opening_round_duration_ms: u64,
)

/// Names the launch's Blast partner, one new launches may name. From `finalize` on it is the
/// launch's partner for good, paid its share of every fee Blast takes on the launch when Blast
/// claims them.
public fun set_partner<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    config: &launch_config::LaunchConfig,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Checks every setting, adds Blast's allocation to the plan, fixes the canonical supply in the
/// Coin Registry, and splits the Meme into the curve reserve and the migration allocations.
/// `creation_fee` must be exactly the configured fee; it joins Blast's fees on the launch. The
/// version check also rejects stale finalization in a transaction that used current intake. The
/// launch's ID derives from `registry` and the coin type.
public fun finalize<Meme, Quote>(
    self: LaunchInitializer<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    registry: &mut LaunchRegistry,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    config: &launch_config::LaunchConfig,
    creation_fee: Coin<sui::SUI>,
): Launch<Meme, Quote>

/// Shares the launch. Public time starts now: trading, or the opening round, which ends
/// `opening_round_ms` later.
public fun share<Meme, Quote>(
    self: Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Authenticates the creator recipient and issues both creator capabilities.
public fun claim_creator_caps<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    config: &runtime_config::RuntimeConfig,
    account_attestation: Option<vector<u8>>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): (creator_authority::CurveFeeCap<Meme>, creator_authority::CreatorMetadataCap<Meme>)

/// Changes only this launch's curve-fee cap delay, after its applied delay.
/// Apply through `apply_curve_fee_cap_delay`; replaces any pending reassignment.
public fun schedule_curve_fee_cap_delay<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CreatorAdmin>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Announces a replacement recipient without invalidating the current curve-fee cap, after
/// the applied delay. Unclaimed curve fees follow the fresh cap when applied.
public fun schedule_curve_fee_cap_reassignment<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CreatorAdmin>,
    recipient: address,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels the pending reassignment or delay change without affecting either live creator cap.
public fun cancel_curve_fee_cap_reassignment<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)

/// Permissionlessly applies a ready curve-fee cap delay change.
public fun apply_curve_fee_cap_delay<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Permissionlessly applies a ready reassignment: issues a fresh curve-fee cap to the
/// recipient and retires the old authority.
public fun apply_curve_fee_cap_reassignment<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Changes only this launch's metadata cap delay, after its applied delay.
/// Apply through `apply_creator_metadata_cap_delay`; replaces any pending reassignment.

    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    creator_metadata_cap: &creator_authority::CreatorMetadataCap<Meme>,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    icon_url: String,
)

/// Adds `quote_in` to the sender's opening-round bid. Before share only the creator's own
/// transaction can reach the launch, so the creator's bid joins the same pot at the same price.
public fun join_opening_round<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    quote_in: Coin<Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Closes the ended opening round: buys from the curve once with the whole pot, at the usual fees
/// and graduation cap, and holds the Meme bought and any Quote refunded for delivery. A pot too
/// small to buy one Meme atom is held whole for refund unless it reaches graduation. Anyone may settle.
public fun settle_opening_round<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    runtime_config: &runtime_config::RuntimeConfig,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Sends `bidder` its share of the settled opening round: the Meme bought and the Quote refunded,
/// pro rata to its bid among the undelivered bids and rounded down, so the last delivery takes the
/// remainder. Anyone may deliver, in any later lifecycle.
public fun deliver_opening_round<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    bidder: address,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Buys Meme with Quote; Quote beyond the graduation target is refunded. Trading opens at share,
/// or at settlement after an opening round; before share only the creator's transaction can buy.
/// A target-reaching buy may return zero Meme when `min_meme_out` is zero.
public fun buy<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    runtime_config: &runtime_config::RuntimeConfig,
    quote_in: Coin<Quote>,
    min_meme_out: u64,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): (Coin<Meme>, Coin<Quote>)

/// Sells Meme for Quote; the sell-burn share of the input waits in `pending_burn`.
public fun sell<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    runtime_config: &runtime_config::RuntimeConfig,
    meme_in: Coin<Meme>,
    min_quote_out: u64,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): Coin<Quote>

public fun claim_creator_fees<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    curve_fee_cap: &creator_authority::CurveFeeCap<Meme>,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): (Coin<Meme>, Coin<Quote>)

/// Pays `treasury` Blast's fees on a launch that names no partner: its trade fees, the creation
/// fee, and the one-time migration fee. Anyone may claim; an empty ledger pays nothing.
public fun claim_protocol_fees<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Pays Blast's fees on a launch that names `partner`: the partner its share of each, then
/// `treasury` the rest, in the same call. Anyone may claim; an empty ledger pays nothing.
public fun claim_protocol_fees_with_partner<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Pays every project recipient, outright or into its vesting schedule starting now, accrues
/// Blast's one-time Meme and Quote fee in the launch ledger, burns the curve's unsold Meme and
/// pending sell-burn (or sends them to `@0x0` when the supply is fixed), and returns the hand-off
/// only the stored venue can redeem: the pool's share and the vault's terms, with Blast's applied
/// LP-fee share and the migration residuals paid to Blast.
/// Anyone may call it; an unredeemed hand-off aborts the transaction.
public fun migrate<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    config: &launch_config::LaunchConfig,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): blast_core::migration::Migration<Meme, Quote>

/// Proves to the launch's vault that the caller holds the curve-fee cap this launch honors.
public fun prove_owner<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &Launch<Meme, Quote>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    curve_fee_cap: &creator_authority::CurveFeeCap<Meme>,
): blast_core::owner_proof::OwnerProof
```

#### Constants

- `DEFAULT_CAP_REASSIGNMENT_DELAY_MS: u64 = 48 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: Initial delay for each creator-cap reassignment: 48 hours in milliseconds.

### `blast_fun::launch_config`

Launch policy each new launch snapshots.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/launchpad/sources/config/launch_config.move

#### Types

```move
/// The two trade rates change atomically.
public struct TradeFees has copy, drop, store {
    quote_fee_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
    meme_fee_bps: blast_core::bps::Bps,
}

/// Admitted decimals, Quotes, and venues; the trade fees; Blast's one-time migration allocation;
/// Blast's share of each new vault's LP fees; the SUI every launch pays at creation; and the Blast
/// partners new launches may name. Each creator picks the virtual Quote reserve their curve
/// starts from.

    id: UID,

    partners: blast_partners::partner_list::PartnerList,

/// Admits a venue's witness type after the applied admission delay. An admitted venue's tickets
/// start launches, and only that venue redeems their migrations; existing launches keep their
/// venue. Rescheduling a pending admission restarts its delay.
///
/// Primitive and framework types are rejected because anyone can construct them.
public fun schedule_venue<Venue: drop>(
    self: &mut LaunchConfig,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,

)

)

    self: &mut LaunchConfig,

    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)

/// Immediately removes a venue for new launches and cancels any pending admission.
public fun remove_venue<Venue: drop>(
    self: &mut LaunchConfig,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
)

/// Proposes only the admission delay. Pending admissions keep their deadlines.
public fun schedule_venue_delay(
    self: &mut LaunchConfig,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Permissionlessly applies a ready admission-delay proposal.
public fun apply_venue_delay(
    self: &mut LaunchConfig,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels only the admission-delay proposal.
public fun cancel_venue_delay(
    self: &mut LaunchConfig,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)

/// Clears the requested pause bits once every launchpad delay and the core treasury's delay are
/// guarded: nonzero, with no pending change that would set one to zero and no pending admission.
/// A delay left at its zero publication value therefore blocks release instead of going live; the
/// generic administration then checks the upgrade delay and the pinned witness. Claims and
/// opening-round delivery never require unpausing; settling an opening round needs the trading
/// bit clear.
public fun unpause(
    self: &LaunchConfig,
    package_version: &mut blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    unpause_admin: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_admin::package_admin::UnpauseAdmin<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>>,
    package_admin: &blast_admin::package_admin::PackageAdmin<blast_fun::BLAST_FUN>,
    runtime_config: &runtime_config::RuntimeConfig,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    bits: u8,
)
```
