## API reference: `blast_migrators` (Cetus and Bluefin venues)

Not yet published on mainnet. Its interface may change before publication.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidCoinOrder` | `CoinA` and `CoinB` must follow the Cetus coin order. |
| 1 | `EPositionPoolMismatch` | The pool must be the vault's own pool. |
| 2 | `EWrongCoinPair` | `CoinA` and `CoinB` must be exactly the sale's Meme and Quote. |
| 3 | `EInvalidTier` | A Bluefin tier needs a tick spacing in 1..=400 and a fee rate in 1..=20,000. |
| 4 | `ESymbolTooLong` | A Bluefin Meme symbol must be at most 32 bytes. |

### `blast_migrators::blast_migrators`

The migrators package's publication.  `init` turns the one-time witness into `blast_admin`'s owned `Publication`, which the publisher wraps with the package's `UpgradeCap` through `blast_admin::package_admin::wrap`; that shares `PackageVersion<BLAST_MIGRATORS>` and `PackageAdmin<BLAST_MIGRATORS>`. Venues define no pause bits, so the version starts unpaused and the package has no unpause. Residual risk: the package has no emergency stop of its own. Migration stops with each product's migration pause bit and `buyback_and_burn` with the vault's admin bit, but `prepare`, `collect_fees`, and `settle_and_apply_fee_split` can be stopped only by advancing this package's version through a delayed upgrade, which retires every call to the old bytecode. Upgrades are scheduled, authorized and committed through the generic `package_admin` under this package's own `UpgradeAdmin<BLAST_MIGRATORS>` role. The products never import this package: each venue redeems the `Migration` its product's own `migrate` returned earlier in the transaction.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/migrators/sources/blast_migrators.move

#### Types

```move
/// The package's one-time witness (OTW), typing its version, administration, and roles.
public struct BLAST_MIGRATORS() has drop;

/// The package witness `blast_admin` pins at publication, the one type that authorizes this
/// package's own administration calls such as unpause. Only this module could construct it, and it
/// is never stored or returned; the package defines no pause bits, so nothing constructs it.
public struct PackageWitness() has drop;
```

### `blast_migrators::bluefin`

Bluefin coin preparation, migration into `blast_vault` custody, fee collection with auto-LP, settled fee-split updates, and buybacks over custodied concentrated-liquidity principal, for launches and presales alike.  Bluefin pools are keyed by their exact type-parameter order, so this module always creates and expects `Pool<Meme, Quote>` with Meme as coin A.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/migrators/sources/venues/bluefin.move

#### Types

```move
/// The Bluefin venue's sealed witness: it issues tickets, redeems migrations, and keys the position
/// in the vault, so each product's venue list and the vault registry admit this one type.
public struct Venue() has drop;

/// A prepared coin's Bluefin pool tier.
public struct PoolTier has copy, drop, store {
    tick_spacing: u32,
    fee_rate: u64,
}

/// Sealed `VenueConfig` field name for the Meme type's Bluefin pool tier.
public struct PoolTierKey<phantom Meme>() has copy, drop, store;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Prepares `Meme` for a Bluefin sale in `Quote` at the creator's tier, recording the tier, and
/// returns the ticket the product's `new` redeems. `treasury_cap` proves the caller controls the
/// coin, so no one else can record its tier first.
///
/// Bluefin checks the tier only when migration creates the pool, and a sale cannot change its
/// tier, so the same bounds apply here first. The pool and its position store the Meme symbol, so
/// it is bounded here too.
public fun prepare<Meme, Quote>(
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    venue_config: &mut venue_config::VenueConfig,
    _: &TreasuryCap<Meme>,
    currency: &Currency<Meme>,
    tick_spacing: u32,
    fee_rate: u64,
): blast_core::migration::VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>

/// Migrates a sale into a new full-range pool at the recorded tier and a `blast_vault` vault:
/// `migration` is the hand-off the launch's or presale's own `migrate` returned earlier in the
/// transaction.
///
/// Redeems the hand-off. The opening price derives from the handed-off balances, which
/// seed the position on the side `is_meme_seed_side` chooses. `creation_fee` is paid whole, and
/// Bluefin aborts unless it is exactly the configured SUI fee. The position and the venue
/// residuals go to the vault, which has no withdrawal path. The caller receives no object; Bluefin
/// records it as the new pool's manager, a role limited to the pool's reward emissions and to
/// handing the role on (ADR 0088).
public fun migrate<Meme, Quote>(
    migration: blast_core::migration::Migration<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    venue_config: &venue_config::VenueConfig,
    currency: &Currency<Meme>,
    quote_currency: &Currency<Quote>,
    bluefin_config: &mut config::GlobalConfig,
    registry: &mut blast_vault::blast_vault_package::VaultRegistry,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    creation_fee: Coin<sui::SUI>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Collects the custodied position's fees, splits them, burns the burn share's Meme, and adds the
/// auto-LP share back to the position. Anyone may call it.
public fun collect_fees<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Anyone may settle accrued fees under the current split, then apply the stored ready proposal.
public fun settle_and_apply_fee_split<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Adds the vault's waiting auto-LP Meme and Quote to its position in `pool` at the current price
/// and keeps the rest for the next call. Anyone may call it, apart from collection, so a Bluefin
/// refusal of the add never blocks collecting fees.
public fun compound_auto_lp<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Swaps `quote_in` of the vault's burn Quote for Meme in its pool and burns it, under a buyback
/// admin. Aborts unless `quote_in` is positive and at most the burn Quote held, and unless the swap
/// returns at least `min_meme_out`; the admin sets it from an off-chain price, since anyone can move
/// the pool's price within a transaction. Quote the pool does not take stays for the next buyback.
public fun buyback_and_burn<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    admin_witness: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::BuybackAdmin>,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    bluefin_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<Meme, Quote>,
    quote_in: u64,
    min_meme_out: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)
```

### `blast_migrators::cetus`

Cetus coin preparation, pool-creation reservation, migration into `blast_vault` custody, and collection, for launches and presales alike.  Anyone holding Meme can create a Cetus pool, and Cetus allows one pool per pair and tick spacing, so a buyer could pre-create the migration pool at a price of their choosing. Preparing the coin therefore mints the Meme type's single Cetus `PoolCreationCap` from its treasury capability, before any Meme can leave the sale's custody, registers the pair at the creator's tick spacing, and returns a `VenueTicket` for that pair the sale redeems; afterwards only that capability or Cetus's pool-manager role can create the pair's pool there. Trusting that role is a venue-operator assumption: a pool it creates first makes the pair's migration abort, and only a migrators upgrade could recover the sale. The capability stays in `VenueConfig` under a key only this module constructs, with no extraction or unregistration path.  Migration redeems the `Migration` the sale's own `migrate` returned earlier in the transaction and burns the full-range position through Cetus LP Burn, so Cetus itself records the sale's liquidity as permanently locked; the `blast_vault` vault keeps the `CetusLPBurnProof`, which can only collect fees. A burned position cannot receive liquidity, so auto-LP adds to a second full-range position the vault opens on first use and keeps with no removal path.  Cetus orders a pool's coin types by type name. Each route after `prepare` takes the pool's coin types as `CoinA` and `CoinB` beside the sale's `Meme` and `Quote`, proves they are the same pair, and retypes balances between the two orders through a checked dynamic-field round trip (ADR 0100).

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/migrators/sources/venues/cetus.move

#### Types

```move
/// The Cetus venue's sealed witness: it issues tickets, redeems migrations, and keys the position
/// in the vault, so each product's venue list and the vault registry admit this one type.
public struct Venue() has drop;

/// Sealed `VenueConfig` object-field name for the Meme type's Cetus `PoolCreationCap`.
///
/// The capability is a dynamic object field, so it keeps the ID that Cetus's permission registry
/// and every `CetusPoolReserved` event record, and stays readable by that ID.
public struct CreationCapKey<phantom Meme>() has copy, drop, store;

/// Sealed `VenueConfig` field name for the Meme type's Cetus tick spacing.
public struct PoolTierKey<phantom Meme>() has copy, drop, store;

/// Transient dynamic-field name `cast` uses to retype a coin or balance.
public struct CastKey() has copy, drop, store;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Prepares `Meme` for a Cetus sale in `Quote` at the creator's `tick_spacing`, reserving Cetus
/// pool creation for the pair, and returns the ticket the product's `new` redeems.
///
/// The Meme type's single `PoolCreationCap` is minted from `treasury_cap` before any Meme can
/// leave the sale's custody, the pair is registered at `tick_spacing`, and the spacing is recorded.
/// Aborts, leaving Cetus unchanged, when Cetus does not register the spacing or refuses the pair
/// at it, so the creator can choose another tier or venue instead.
public fun prepare<Meme, Quote>(
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    venue_config: &mut venue_config::VenueConfig,
    cetus_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pools: &mut factory::Pools,
    treasury_cap: &mut TreasuryCap<Meme>,
    tick_spacing: u32,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): blast_core::migration::VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>

/// Migrates a sale into a Cetus pool and a `blast_vault` vault: `migration` is the hand-off the
/// launch's or presale's own `migrate` returned earlier in the transaction. `CoinA` and `CoinB`
/// name the sale's Meme and Quote in Cetus's canonical coin order.
///
/// Redeems the hand-off, creates the pair's pool with the reserved capability at the recorded
/// tick spacing, priced from the handed-off balances, seeds full-range liquidity with exactly
/// those balances on the side `is_coin_a_seed_side` chooses, burns the position, and creates the
/// vault from the sale's terms with the burn proof and Cetus's residual. Any Cetus refusal,
/// including a tier Cetus no longer registers, aborts the whole transaction, the product's
/// `migrate` with it, so the sale stays ready to migrate.
public fun migrate<Meme, Quote, CoinA, CoinB>(
    migration: blast_core::migration::Migration<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    venue_config: &venue_config::VenueConfig,
    cetus_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pools: &mut factory::Pools,
    burn_manager: &mut lp_burn::BurnManager,
    registry: &mut blast_vault::blast_vault_package::VaultRegistry,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Collects the vault position's fees from its pool, splits them, and burns the burn share's Meme;
/// the auto-LP share waits in the vault for `compound_auto_lp`. Anyone may call it. `CoinA` and
/// `CoinB` are the pool's coin order.
public fun collect_fees<Meme, Quote, CoinA, CoinB>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    cetus_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    burn_manager: &lp_burn::BurnManager,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<CoinA, CoinB>,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Anyone may settle accrued fees under the current split, then apply the stored ready proposal. `CoinA`
/// and `CoinB` are the pool's coin order.
public fun settle_and_apply_fee_split<Meme, Quote, CoinA, CoinB>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    treasury: &blast_core::blast_treasury::Treasury,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    cetus_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    burn_manager: &lp_burn::BurnManager,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<CoinA, CoinB>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Adds the vault's waiting auto-LP Meme and Quote to its auto-LP position in `pool` at the
/// current price and keeps the rest for the next call. Anyone may call it, apart from collection,
/// so a Cetus refusal of the add never blocks collecting fees. `CoinA` and `CoinB` are the pool's
/// coin order.
public fun compound_auto_lp<Meme, Quote, CoinA, CoinB>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    cetus_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<CoinA, CoinB>,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Swaps `quote_in` of the vault's burn Quote for Meme in its pool and burns it, under a buyback
/// admin. Aborts unless `quote_in` is positive and at most the burn Quote held, and unless the swap
/// returns at least `min_meme_out`; the admin sets it from an off-chain price, since anyone can move
/// the pool's price within a transaction. Quote the pool does not take stays for the next buyback.
/// `CoinA` and `CoinB` are the pool's coin order.
public fun buyback_and_burn<Meme, Quote, CoinA, CoinB>(
    self: &mut blast_vault::vault::Vault<Meme, Quote>,
    migrators_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_migrators::BLAST_MIGRATORS>,
    vault_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<blast_vault::blast_vault_package::BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    admin_witness: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::BuybackAdmin>,
    currency: &mut Currency<Meme>,
    cetus_config: &config::GlobalConfig,
    pool: &mut pool::Pool<CoinA, CoinB>,
    quote_in: u64,
    min_meme_out: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)
```

### `blast_migrators::constants`

Package-wide migrator constants exposed as zero-cost macros.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/migrators/sources/lib/constants.move

#### Constants

- `version: u64 = 1`: The package version this bytecode serves.
- `bluefin_max_tick_spacing: u32 = 400`: Bluefin pool creation's own tick-spacing bound (`pool` aborts 1026 above it).
- `bluefin_max_fee_rate: u64 = 20_000`: Bluefin pool creation's own fee-rate bound (`pool` aborts 1027 above it).
- `max_meme_symbol_length: u64 = 32`: Longest Meme symbol, in bytes, a Bluefin pool accepts: the pool and its position store the symbol, and the pool name repeats it, so a longer one would make the position too large.
- `min_swap_sqrt_price: u128 = 4_295_048_017`: Lowest sqrt price a Cetus or Bluefin swap accepts as its limit; buybacks selling Quote as coin A run to it and rely on the caller's minimum Meme out instead.
- `max_swap_sqrt_price: u128 = 79_226_673_515_401_279_992_447_579_054`: Highest sqrt price a Cetus or Bluefin swap accepts as its limit, for buybacks selling Quote as coin B.
- `q64_shift: u8 = 64`: Fractional bits of a Q64.64 sqrt price.
- `q128_shift: u8 = 128`: Fractional bits of a Q128 value; a fixed-A auto-LP liquidity must stay below it.
- `auto_lp_margin: u256 = 2`: Atoms of the other asset kept in reserve beyond the estimate, covering the venue's own rounding of the liquidity it derives from the fixed amount.

### `blast_migrators::events`

Event payloads and their package-only emitters for `blast_migrators`: the pool tier each venue records while preparing a pair. Vault creation, LP-fee collection, auto-LP, buybacks, and holder rewards emit from `blast_vault`; versions and upgrades from `blast_admin`.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/migrators/sources/lib/events.move

#### Events

```move
/// Cetus pool creation for the pair is reserved under `pool_creation_cap_id`, the Meme type's one
/// `PoolCreationCap`, at the tick spacing its migration uses.
public struct CetusPoolReserved<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    pool_creation_cap_id: ID,
    tick_spacing: u32,
}

/// The Bluefin pool tier the pair's migration uses.
public struct BluefinPoolTierRecorded<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    tick_spacing: u32,
    fee_rate: u64,
}
```

### `blast_migrators::venue_config`

Shared venue state: write-once entries only their venue module can key.  Venue modules add authority-bearing objects (the Meme type's Cetus `PoolCreationCap`) as dynamic object fields, so each object keeps its ID and stays readable by it, and plain records (a prepared coin's pool tier) as dynamic fields, while preparing a coin. No entry is ever removed, replaced, or borrowed mutably. New venue modules add entries without changing this object's layout.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/migrators/sources/venues/venue_config.move

#### Types

```move
/// Shared singleton holding venue-owned entries.
public struct VenueConfig has key {
    id: UID,
}
```

## API reference: `blast_core` (Shared core)

Not yet published on mainnet. Its interface may change before publication.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidBps` | A basis-points rate must be at most 10,000. |
| 1 | `EInvalidApportionment` | Apportioned rates must sum to 10,000 and name a rounded-up part inside them. |
| 2 | `EInvalidVestingTerms` | Vesting needs positive steps whose total and cliff fit the technical bound. |
| 3 | `EInvalidRoute` | A route needs a positive share and a nonzero address. |
| 4 | `EInvalidFeeSplit` | A fee split needs at most 20 distinct routes summing to 10,000. |
| 5 | `EInvalidTreasury` | The treasury must be a nonzero address. |
| 6 | `EInvalidPair` | The Meme and Quote types must differ. |
| 7 | `EPartnerMismatch` | Blast's fees pay with exactly the partner the sale named, or with none. |
| 8 | `EWrongVenue` | Only the venue the sale stored can redeem its migration. |
| 9 | `EWrongOwner` | The owner proof must name the vault's package witness and sale. |
| 10 | `EWrongPackageWitness` | The vault terms must be sealed by the package witness creating the migration. |
| 11 | `EInvalidLpFeeBps` | Blast's LP-fee share of each asset must be at most 3,000 basis points. |
| 12 | `EInvalidRecipient` | A payout recipient must be a nonzero address. |
| 13 | `ELedgerNotEmpty` | A ledger must hold no fees to be destroyed. |

### `blast_core::blast_core_events`

Event payloads and their package-only emitters for `blast_core`.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/lib/blast_core_events.move

#### Events

```move
/// Blast's held fees were paid from the ledger of `origin`, created under `package_witness`: the
/// treasury's amounts, and the partner's cut when a partner is bound.
public struct ProtocolFeesPaid<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    package_witness: TypeName,
    origin: ID,
    treasury: address,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
    sui_amount: u64,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
    partner_meme_amount: u64,
    partner_quote_amount: u64,
    partner_sui_amount: u64,
}
```

### `blast_core::blast_domain`

Blast's product administration roles in the shared Blast ACL, one set for the launchpad, the presale, the vault, and the venues (ADR 0137). Package-infrastructure roles (upgrade, pause, unpause) are generic over each package's one-time witness (OTW) in `blast_admin`.  A role is a type. An `AdminCap` holding `Role` signs in to `AdminWitness<Role>`, and a function's witness parameter names the one role it accepts, so no role authorizes another's actions.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/blast_domain.move

#### Types

```move
/// Chooses where Blast is paid.
public struct TreasuryAdmin {}

/// Sets Blast's fees and shares and the partners new sales may name.
public struct FeeAdmin {}

/// Admits or removes the Quotes and Meme decimals new sales may use.
public struct ListingAdmin {}

/// Admits or removes venues and vault issuers.
public struct VenueAdmin {}

/// Schedules holder-reward rounds and settles their gas reserves.
public struct RewardsAdmin {}

/// Swaps a vault's burn Quote for Meme to burn.
public struct BuybackAdmin {}

/// Revokes and reissues a launch's creator capabilities.
public struct CreatorAdmin {}

/// Proposes and revokes the keys that may sign account claims.
public struct ClaimSignerAdmin {}

/// May cancel any scheduled product administrative action.
public struct CancelAdmin {}
```

### `blast_core::blast_treasury`

The one address every Blast fee is paid to, for every product and vault. A `TreasuryAdmin` schedules a change behind the applied delay, a `CancelAdmin` cancels it, and anyone applies a ready one.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/blast_treasury.move

#### Types

```move
/// Shared singleton holding the protocol destination. It has no destroy: every ledger pays
/// through it, so deleting it would strand Blast's held fees in every launch, presale, and vault.
public struct Treasury has key {
    id: UID,
    destination: blast_admin::delayed::Delayed<DestinationKey, address>,
}

/// Keys the destination's delayed lifecycle.
public struct DestinationKey() has copy, drop, store;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Schedules a new nonzero destination and next delay, ready after the applied delay.
public fun schedule_destination(
    self: &mut Treasury,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_domain::TreasuryAdmin>,
    destination: address,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies the scheduled destination once ready.
public fun apply_destination(self: &mut Treasury, clock: &Clock)

/// Cancels the pending change, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_destination(
    self: &mut Treasury,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
)

/// The address Blast's fees are paid to now.
public fun destination(self: &Treasury): address

/// The delay the next change waits, in milliseconds.
public fun delay_ms(self: &Treasury): u64

/// The scheduled destination with its next delay and ready time, if any.
public fun pending_destination(self: &Treasury): Option<blast_admin::delayed::Proposal<address>>

/// Whether `apply_destination` would succeed now: a destination is scheduled, its ready time has
/// passed, and its next delay still fits in a Unix millisecond timestamp.
public fun is_destination_ready(self: &Treasury, clock: &Clock): bool

/// Whether every change waits a nonzero delay.
public fun is_guarded(self: &Treasury): bool
```

### `blast_core::bps`

Validated basis-points rates, their rounded application, and the one exact apportionment every Blast split uses.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/accounting/bps.move

#### Types

```move
/// A rate from zero through 10,000 basis points; `new` is the only way to build one.
public struct Bps(u64) has copy, drop, store;
```

#### Functions

```move
/// A rate of `value` basis points; aborts above 10,000.
public fun new(value: u64): Bps

/// The rate in basis points.
public fun value(self: Bps): u64

/// Applies the rate to `amount`, rounding up; the result is at most `amount`.
public fun apply_up(self: Bps, amount: u64): u64

/// Largest-remainder apportionment of `total` over `rates`, which must sum to exactly 10,000: the
/// `rounded_up` part takes its ceiling, every other part its floor, and the leftover units go one
/// each to the largest remaining remainders, earlier parts first. The amounts sum to `total`.
public fun apportion(rates: vector<Bps>, total: u64, rounded_up: Option<u64>): vector<u64>
```

### `blast_core::constants`

Core constants exposed as zero-cost macros. The basis-points denominator, Blast's LP-fee share maximum, and the bounds of core's public constructors are public, so every launch package uses the one value.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/lib/constants.move

#### Functions

```move
/// Denominator for rates expressed in basis points.
public fun basis_points_denominator(): u64

/// Blast's largest share of each asset in a vault's LP fees, in basis points. `vault_terms`
/// enforces it when a sale or issuer seals a vault's terms.
public fun max_lp_fee_bps(): u64

/// Maximum routes in one fee split, so 16 recipients still fit beside the four special kinds.
/// Every collection pays them in the same transaction.
public fun max_routes(): u64

/// A technical bound on a vesting schedule's duration: from any clock below 2^63 ms, about 292
/// million years, the schedule's end still fits in u64, so payout cannot overflow creating it.
public fun max_vesting_duration_ms(): u64
```

#### Constants

- `digest_length: u64 = 32`: Byte length of a Blake2b-256 digest: Merkle roots, leaves, and proof siblings.
- `min_sqrt_price_x64: u128 = 4_295_048_016`: The lowest sqrt price, Q64.64, the Cetus and Bluefin `tick_math` modules accept.
- `max_sqrt_price_x64: u128 = 79_226_673_515_401_279_992_447_579_055`: The highest sqrt price, Q64.64, the Cetus and Bluefin `tick_math` modules accept.
- `initial_treasury_delay_ms: u64 = 48 * 60 * 60 * 1_000`: PendingDelay every treasury change waits from publication: the genesis destination is compiled, so no setup needs a zero delay.

### `blast_core::fee_split`

How every LP-fee collection is divided after Blast's share: typed routes to addresses, the vault's owner, auto-LP, holder rewards, and burn, summing to exactly 10,000 basis points.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/accounting/fee_split.move

#### Types

```move
/// Where one route's part of each collection goes. The vault decides what each special kind does
/// with it: the owner claims its part, auto-LP adds liquidity, holder rewards fund rounds, and burn
/// burns Meme and buys Meme back with Quote to burn.
public enum Destination has copy, drop, store {
    Address(address),
    Owner,
    AutoLp,
    HolderRewards,
    Burn,
}

/// One route's destination and positive share.
public struct Route has copy, drop, store {
    destination: Destination,
    bps: bps::Bps,
}

/// Validated routes: at most 20, each positive, no address twice, at most one of each special
/// kind, summing to exactly 10,000. Only `new` builds one, so every split a vault holds assigns
/// each collection in full.
public struct FeeSplit has copy, drop, store {
    routes: vector<Route>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// A route to `destination`; aborts for the zero address.
public fun address_destination(destination: address): Destination

/// A route to the vault's owner, who claims its part with an owner proof.
public fun owner_destination(): Destination

/// A route the vault adds back into its position as liquidity.
public fun auto_lp_destination(): Destination

/// A route that funds the vault's holder-reward rounds.
public fun holder_rewards_destination(): Destination

/// A route whose Meme is burned and whose Quote buys Meme back to burn.
public fun burn_destination(): Destination

/// A route of `bps` to `destination`; aborts for a zero share.
public fun route(destination: Destination, bps: bps::Bps): Route

/// A fee split of `routes`; aborts unless they are at most 20, distinct, and sum to 10,000.
public fun new(routes: vector<Route>): FeeSplit

/// Splits `total` across the routes exactly, in route order.
public fun apportion(self: &FeeSplit, total: u64): vector<u64>

/// The routes, in the order `apportion` pays them.
public fun routes(self: &FeeSplit): &vector<Route>

/// Where the route pays.
public fun destination(self: &Route): Destination

/// The route's share.
public fun bps(self: &Route): bps::Bps

/// The route's address, or none for a special kind.
public fun address_of(self: &Destination): Option<address>

/// Whether the destination is the vault's owner.
public fun is_owner(self: &Destination): bool

/// Whether the destination is auto-LP.
public fun is_auto_lp(self: &Destination): bool

/// Whether the destination is holder rewards.
public fun is_holder_rewards(self: &Destination): bool

/// Whether the destination is burn.
public fun is_burn(self: &Destination): bool
```

### `blast_core::merkle`

Positional Blake2b-256 proofs. Leaves hash under a `0x00` prefix and a consumer's domain string, internal nodes under `0x01`, so no internal node can pass as a leaf.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/merkle.move

#### Functions

```move
/// Hashes one leaf: `blake2b256(0x00 || domain || payload)`. `domain` names the tree's purpose,
/// such as `b"blast-holder-rewards-v1"`; `payload` is the leaf's BCS-encoded fields.
public fun leaf(domain: vector<u8>, payload: vector<u8>): vector<u8>

/// Hashes one ordered pair as an internal node: `blake2b256(0x01 || left || right)`.
public fun node(left: vector<u8>, right: vector<u8>): vector<u8>

/// Verifies a positional proof of `digest` at `index` among `leaf_count` leaves under `root`.
/// Returns false, before hashing, for a malformed root or digest, an empty tree, an index outside
/// it, a proof of the wrong depth, or a malformed sibling.
public fun verify(
    root: vector<u8>,
    digest: vector<u8>,
    index: u64,
    leaf_count: u64,
    proof: &vector<vector<u8>>,
): bool
```

### `blast_core::migration`

The sealed contract between a sale and the venue that migrates it.  Before supply leaves the sale's `TreasuryCap`, a venue prepares the coin and returns a `VenueTicket` sealed by its witness; the product admits and stores the ticket's venue. At migration the product creates a `Migration` sealed by its own witness, carrying the pool's balances and the new vault's terms, and only the stored venue's witness can redeem it. Neither the ticket nor the migration can be dropped, copied, or stored, so a transaction completes the migration whole or aborts.  Every witness is identified by `type_name::with_original_ids`, which stays fixed across upgrades of the venue or product package; allowlists that admit these names must use the same form.  The module also derives the price a concentrated-liquidity venue seeds the pool at, and whether both such venues can seed it, so a product can refuse a sale that could never migrate.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/migration/migration.move

#### Types

```move
/// What happens to the migration residuals the pool does not take.
public enum ResidualPolicy has copy, drop, store {
    /// Paid to Blast through the vault's protocol ledger.
    Protocol,
    /// Held in the vault for the sale's owner to claim.
    Owner,
}

/// Everything a new vault snapshots from the sale that sealed its terms. Sealed terms authorize a
/// vault, so products and venues must not return them by value: a venue takes them only from
/// `redeem`.
public struct VaultTerms has copy, drop, store {
    /// The launch or presale the vault belongs to; owner proofs name it.
    origin: ID,
    /// The original type of the package witness that sealed the terms; owner proofs must name it.
    package_witness: TypeName,
    fee_split: fee_split::FeeSplit,
    /// Blast's share of each LP-fee collection, per asset.
    protocol_meme_bps: bps::Bps,
    protocol_quote_bps: bps::Bps,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
    residuals: ResidualPolicy,
}

/// A venue's sealed ticket for the `Meme` and `Quote` pair it prepared, redeemed by the product
/// that admits it for a sale of exactly that pair. The venue keeps the pair's pool tier and any
/// reserved pool-creation authority itself (ADR 0137).
public struct VenueTicket<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> {
    venue: TypeName,
}

/// One sale's pool balances and vault terms, redeemable only by `venue`.
public struct Migration<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> {
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
    venue: TypeName,
    terms: VaultTerms,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Residuals paid to Blast through the vault's protocol ledger.
public fun residuals_to_protocol(): ResidualPolicy

/// Residuals held in the vault for the sale's owner to claim.
public fun residuals_to_owner(): ResidualPolicy

/// Issues a ticket for the `Meme` and `Quote` pair the venue has prepared, sealed under its
/// witness `Venue`.
public fun issue_ticket<Meme, Quote, Venue: drop>(_: Venue): VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>

/// Redeems a ticket, returning the venue witness type to admit and store.
public fun redeem_ticket<Meme, Quote>(self: VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>): TypeName

/// The original type of the venue witness that issued the ticket. Called as `ticket.venue()`;
/// `redeem_ticket` stays the ticket's one consuming path.
public fun ticket_venue<Meme, Quote>(self: &VenueTicket<Meme, Quote>): TypeName

/// A vault's terms, sealed under the `PackageWitness` of the sale's product that owns `origin`; owner proofs for the vault must come from the same witness. Each Blast share is at
/// most `constants::max_lp_fee_bps!()`, 3,000 basis points, so a vault trusts sealed terms.
public fun vault_terms<PackageWitness: drop>(
    _: PackageWitness,
    origin: ID,
    fee_split: fee_split::FeeSplit,
    protocol_meme_bps: bps::Bps,
    protocol_quote_bps: bps::Bps,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
    residuals: ResidualPolicy,
): VaultTerms

/// Creates a sale's migration under its `PackageWitness`, for the stored `venue`, with terms the
/// same package witness sealed.
public fun new<Meme, Quote, PackageWitness: drop>(
    _: PackageWitness,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
    venue: TypeName,
    terms: VaultTerms,
): Migration<Meme, Quote>

/// Redeems the migration under the stored venue's witness `Venue`, returning the pool's balances
/// and the vault's terms. Aborts for any other venue.
public fun redeem<Meme, Quote, Venue: drop>(
    self: Migration<Meme, Quote>,
    _: Venue,
): (Balance<Meme>, Balance<Quote>, VaultTerms)

/// The original type of the only venue witness that can redeem the migration.
public fun venue<Meme, Quote>(self: &Migration<Meme, Quote>): TypeName

/// The Meme and Quote the migration carries to the pool. Only `redeem` releases them.
public fun balances<Meme, Quote>(self: &Migration<Meme, Quote>): (u64, u64)

/// The vault's terms the migration carries. A copy authorizes nothing: an admitted venue never
/// takes terms from its caller, and each sale has one vault.
public fun terms<Meme, Quote>(self: &Migration<Meme, Quote>): &VaultTerms

/// The launch or presale the vault belongs to.
public fun origin(self: &VaultTerms): ID

/// The original type of the package witness that sealed the terms.
public fun package_witness(self: &VaultTerms): TypeName

/// The fee split the vault starts with.
public fun fee_split(self: &VaultTerms): fee_split::FeeSplit

/// Blast's share of each LP-fee collection, Meme then Quote.
public fun protocol_bps(self: &VaultTerms): (bps::Bps, bps::Bps)

/// The partner the sale named, if any.
public fun partner_id(self: &VaultTerms): Option<ID>

/// What happens to the migration residuals.
public fun residuals(self: &VaultTerms): ResidualPolicy

/// Whether the policy pays residuals to Blast.
public fun is_protocol(self: &ResidualPolicy): bool

/// Whether the policy holds residuals for the sale's owner.
public fun is_owner(self: &ResidualPolicy): bool

/// The pool's seed price from the migration's atomic amounts, `floor(sqrt(B / A) * 2^64)` in the
/// Q64.64 form concentrated-liquidity venues take: B / A is Quote / Meme when Meme is coin A, and
/// Meme / Quote otherwise. Display decimals never enter it. A zero divisor amount (Meme when it
/// is coin A, Quote otherwise) aborts with an arithmetic error.
public fun sqrt_price_x64(meme_amount: u64, quote_amount: u64, meme_is_coin_a: bool): u128

/// Whether Cetus and Bluefin can both seed a full-range pool at `sqrt_price_x64`. Both venues
/// share the same `tick_math` sqrt-price bounds; halving the range keeps the price inside any
/// full range aligned inward to a tick spacing. The bounds are reciprocal, so either coin order
/// agrees.
public fun is_seedable_price(sqrt_price_x64: u128): bool
```

### `blast_core::owner_proof`

A package's sealed statement that the caller holds its creator authority over one sale, so a vault can serve the sale's owner without importing either product. Each product keeps its own authority (a curve-fee cap, a presale cap) and issues the proof after checking it; the proof cannot be dropped, copied, or stored, so it authorizes exactly the one call that consumes it.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/migration/owner_proof.move

#### Types

```move
/// The caller holds the creator authority of sale `origin`, as the witness's package attests.
public struct OwnerProof {
    package_witness: TypeName,
    origin: ID,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Issues a proof for the sale `origin` under the issuing package's witness `PackageWitness`.
public fun new<PackageWitness: drop>(_: PackageWitness, origin: ID): OwnerProof

/// Consumes the proof, aborting unless it names `terms`' package witness and sale.
public fun assert_terms(self: OwnerProof, terms: &migration::VaultTerms)

/// The original type of the package witness that issued the proof.
public fun package_witness(self: &OwnerProof): TypeName

/// The sale whose creator authority the proof attests.
public fun origin(self: &OwnerProof): ID
```

### `blast_core::payout`

The one way Blast sends coins to people: an amount paid outright, or locked in an irrevocable linear vesting schedule. A `Recipient` says which of the two a destination gets; each product keeps its own recipient lists and shares.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/accounting/payout.move

#### Types

```move
/// Linear vesting terms: `periods` equal steps of `period_duration_ms` each, the first available
/// `cliff_delay_ms` after the payout. The whole schedule fits in `max_vesting_duration_ms`.
public struct VestingTerms has copy, drop, store {
    cliff_delay_ms: u64,
    period_duration_ms: u64,
    periods: u64,
}

/// Where a payout goes and how: outright, or locked under `vesting_terms` from the moment it is
/// paid.
public struct Recipient has copy, drop, store {
    destination: address,
    vesting_terms: Option<VestingTerms>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Linear vesting terms whose steps and cliff fit in the technical bound, so a payout can always
/// create the schedule. Product limits on the duration belong to creation tooling.
public fun vesting_terms(cliff_delay_ms: u64, period_duration_ms: u64, periods: u64): VestingTerms

/// A recipient at the nonzero `destination`, paid outright without `vesting_terms` and locked
/// under them otherwise.
public fun recipient(destination: address, vesting_terms: Option<VestingTerms>): Recipient

/// The address the recipient's payouts go to, or vest for.
public fun destination(self: &Recipient): address

/// The terms the recipient's payouts vest under, or none when they are paid outright. Called as
/// `recipient.vesting_terms()`.
public fun recipient_vesting_terms(self: &Recipient): Option<VestingTerms>

/// The delay from a payout to its first available step, in milliseconds.
public fun cliff_delay_ms(self: &VestingTerms): u64

/// The length of each equal step, in milliseconds.
public fun period_duration_ms(self: &VestingTerms): u64

/// The number of equal steps.
public fun periods(self: &VestingTerms): u64

/// Pays `amount` of `balance` to `recipient`: outright, or locked in an irrevocable linear
/// schedule starting now when it vests, returning the schedule's ID. A zero amount sends and
/// locks nothing.
public fun pay_or_vest<Asset>(
    balance: &mut Balance<Asset>,
    amount: u64,
    recipient: &Recipient,
    clock: &Clock,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
): Option<ID>

/// Transfers `amount` split from `balance` to `destination` as a coin; a zero amount sends
/// nothing.
public fun pay<Asset>(
    balance: &mut Balance<Asset>,
    amount: u64,
    destination: address,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Transfers all of `balance` to `destination`; an empty balance sends nothing.
public fun pay_all<Asset>(balance: Balance<Asset>, destination: address, ctx: &mut TxContext)
```

### `blast_core::protocol_fees`

Blast's fees held by one launch, presale, or vault, and their one payout path: to the treasury, and, when the sale named a partner, the partner's cut of every asset first.  Only the module that defines the `PackageWitness` can create a ledger, and the payout event names that witness's original type, so a fee payment's origin is attributable to the product or vault that holds it. The embedding object owns the ledger, so only its module can accrue into it or pay it out; each product wraps the payout in its own permissionless call.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/core/sources/accounting/protocol_fees.move

#### Types

```move
/// Blast's Meme, Quote, and SUI fees, held until paid. `partner_id` is fixed at creation: a ledger
/// bound to a partner pays only with that partner, so Blast's fees never skip the partner's cut.
public struct Ledger<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has store {
    /// The original type of the package witness that created the ledger.
    package_witness: TypeName,
    origin: ID,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
    sui: Balance<sui::SUI>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// An empty ledger for the object `origin`, created under the embedding module's `PackageWitness`
/// and bound to `partner_id` if any.
public fun new<Meme, Quote, PackageWitness: drop>(
    _: PackageWitness,
    origin: ID,
    partner_id: Option<ID>,
): Ledger<Meme, Quote>

/// Adds Meme and Quote owned wholly by Blast.
public fun accrue<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Ledger<Meme, Quote>,
    meme: Balance<Meme>,
    quote: Balance<Quote>,
)

/// Adds SUI owned wholly by Blast, such as a creation fee.
public fun accrue_sui<Meme, Quote>(self: &mut Ledger<Meme, Quote>, sui: Balance<sui::SUI>)

/// Takes Blast's cut of one fee in each asset, `meme_bps` and `quote_bps` rounded up, into the
/// ledger and leaves the rest in `meme_fee` and `quote_fee`. Returns the Meme and Quote taken.
public fun take_cut<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Ledger<Meme, Quote>,
    meme_fee: &mut Balance<Meme>,
    quote_fee: &mut Balance<Quote>,
    meme_bps: bps::Bps,
    quote_bps: bps::Bps,
): (u64, u64)

/// Pays everything held to the treasury; an empty ledger sends and emits nothing. Aborts when the
/// ledger is bound to a partner.
public fun pay<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Ledger<Meme, Quote>,
    treasury: &blast_treasury::Treasury,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Pays the bound partner its cut of each asset, then the treasury the rest; an empty ledger sends
/// and emits nothing. Aborts unless `partner` is the ledger's partner.
public fun pay_with_partner<Meme, Quote>(
    self: &mut Ledger<Meme, Quote>,
    treasury: &blast_treasury::Treasury,
    partner: &blast_partners::blast_partners::Partner,
    ctx: &mut TxContext,
)

/// Deletes a ledger that holds nothing; pay it out first. Aborts while any fee is held, so no
/// destruction loses Blast's or the partner's fees.
public fun destroy<Meme, Quote>(self: Ledger<Meme, Quote>)

/// The Meme, Quote, and SUI held.
public fun balances<Meme, Quote>(self: &Ledger<Meme, Quote>): (u64, u64, u64)

/// The original type of the package witness that created the ledger.
public fun package_witness<Meme, Quote>(self: &Ledger<Meme, Quote>): TypeName

/// The launch, presale, or vault whose fees the ledger holds.
public fun origin<Meme, Quote>(self: &Ledger<Meme, Quote>): ID

/// The partner the ledger pays with, fixed at creation, if any.
public fun partner_id<Meme, Quote>(self: &Ledger<Meme, Quote>): Option<ID>
```

## API reference: `blast_vault` (LP vault)

Not yet published on mainnet. Its interface may change before publication.

Events: payload structs are emitted directly, each under its own type.

Abort codes: this package aborts with the numeric codes below from whichever module detects the failure; the abort location names that module.

| Code | Constant | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | `EInvalidWitness` | An admitted witness must not be a primitive or framework type. |
| 1 | `EUnadmittedVenue` | Only an admitted venue may create vaults. |
| 2 | `EUnguardedDelay` | Unpause needs a nonzero witness admission delay and no pending admission. |
| 3 | `EInvalidProtocolBps` | Blast's share must be at most 3,000 basis points per asset. |
| 4 | `EZeroLiquidity` | The migration position must hold liquidity. |
| 5 | `EWrongVenue` | Only the venue holding the vault's position may act on it. |
| 6 | `ENothingToClaim` | The owner has nothing accrued to claim. |
| 7 | `EBuybackQuoteOutOfRange` | Buyback Quote must be positive and at most the burn Quote held. |
| 8 | `EBuybackBelowMinimum` | The buyback must return at least the minimum Meme. |
| 9 | `EInvalidCommitment` | The root and manifest digest must each be 32 bytes. |
| 10 | `EInvalidLeafCount` | A round must commit 1 to 65,536 leaves. |
| 11 | `EEmptyBudget` | A round must fund some Meme or Quote. |
| 12 | `EEmptyInterval` | The start checkpoint must precede the end checkpoint. |
| 13 | `EBudgetOverflow` | The holder Quote and gas reserve must sum within u64. |
| 14 | `EGasReserveTooLarge` | The gas reserve must be at most 10% of the holder Quote. |
| 15 | `EZeroTreasury` | The expected treasury must be a nonzero address. |
| 16 | `EZeroClaimWindow` | The claim window must be positive. |
| 17 | `EWrongRound` | The round number must match the vault's next round. |
| 18 | `EOverlappingInterval` | The checkpoint interval must not overlap the previous round. |
| 19 | `EInsufficientBudget` | Holder funding and gas reserve must fit the vault's unreserved rewards. |
| 20 | `EWrongRoundProposal` | Cancel the pending proposal with the call matching its kind. |
| 21 | `ETreasuryChanged` | The expected treasury must match the treasury's destination. |
| 22 | `EClaimDeadlineOverflow` | The claim window must end within u64 milliseconds. |
| 23 | `EClaimsClosed` | The claim deadline has been reached. |
| 24 | `EInvalidIndex` | The claim index must be less than the committed leaf count. |
| 25 | `EAlreadyClaimed` | This allocation has already been paid. |
| 26 | `EInvalidBeneficiary` | The beneficiary must not be the zero address. |
| 27 | `EEmptyClaim` | At least one claimed amount must be nonzero. |
| 28 | `EInvalidProof` | The proof must match the committed recipient, amounts, and domain. |
| 29 | `EWrongVault` | A round or a venue receipt acts only on its own vault. |
| 30 | `EReserveSettled` | Gas reimbursement is disabled or already settled. |
| 31 | `EOverReserve` | The reimbursement must not exceed the round's gas reserve. |
| 32 | `EClaimsStillOpen` | A round can close only at or after its claim deadline. |
| 33 | `EUnadmittedPackageWitness` | Only terms sealed by an admitted issuer may create vaults. |
| 34 | `EReturnedExceedsTaken` | A venue returns at most what it took from the vault. |

### `blast_vault::blast_vault_events`

Event payloads and their package-only emitters for `blast_vault`. Delayed settings, venue admission, pauses, and versions emit from `blast_admin`; Blast's fee payments emit from `blast_core`.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/vault/sources/lib/blast_vault_events.move

#### Events

```move
/// A venue created and shared a vault with its position and settled its migration residuals.
public struct VaultCreated<phantom Meme, phantom Quote, phantom Venue> has copy, drop {

    pool_id: ID,

    liquidity: u128,

    residual_quote_amount: u64,
}

/// One collection was split: Blast's cut, then each route's part in the applied split's order.
public struct FeesCollected<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    vault_id: ID,
    gross_meme_amount: u64,
    gross_quote_amount: u64,
    protocol_meme_amount: u64,
    protocol_quote_amount: u64,

    route_quote_amounts: vector<u64>,
}

/// The venue attached the vault's auto-LP position.
public struct AutoLpPositionOpened<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    vault_id: ID,
    position_id: ID,
}

/// Auto-LP added liquidity, with what still waits for the next call.

    vault_id: ID,
    meme_amount: u64,
    quote_amount: u64,
}

/// A holder-reward round was published with its terms and the vault's remaining rewards.
public struct RoundPublished<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    vault_id: ID,
    round_id: ID,
    number: u64,
    start_checkpoint: u64,
    end_checkpoint: u64,

    quote_remaining: u64,
}

/// A round's gas reserve was settled, with the vault's unreserved Quote after the return.
public struct GasReserveSettled<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    vault_id: ID,
    round_id: ID,
    number: u64,
    treasury: address,

    quote_unallocated: u64,
}

/// A round closed and returned its remainder to its vault.
public struct RoundClosed<phantom Meme, phantom Quote> has copy, drop {
    vault_id: ID,
    round_id: ID,
    number: u64,
    meme_returned: u64,
    quote_returned: u64,
    gas_reserve_returned: u64,
}
```

### `blast_vault::blast_vault_package`

The vault package's publication, its witness admission, and its unpause.  One list of admitted witness types decides who may create a vault: the venue whose witness calls `vault::new`, and the issuer whose witness sealed the vault's terms, must both be on it. Each witness is the one its module alone can construct. The launchpad and presale witnesses are issuers and the venue witnesses are venues. The list does not distinguish the two roles, so an admitted product witness would also pass the venue check; both are Blast-reviewed code, and products never call `vault::new`. Gating venues binds every position key to venue code Blast reviewed; gating issuers keeps a stranger's terms, with a Blast share and partner of its choosing, out of Blast vaults.  An admitted venue obtains `VaultTerms` only from `migration::redeem`; it never accepts `VaultTerms` from its caller. Terms are `copy`, so a venue that took them as an argument would let anyone who obtained a sale's terms create a second vault claiming that sale.  An `VenueAdmin` schedules an admission, anyone applies it once its delay has passed, a `CancelAdmin` cancels it, and an `VenueAdmin` removes a witness at once. Removing a venue stops it creating vaults; removing an issuer stops every vault for its sales, so a migration sealed by it can no longer complete. Existing vaults keep working either way.  The admission delay starts at zero and admissions ignore the pause, so Blast lists the launchpad, presale, and venue witnesses and raises the delay while publication keeps the package paused; `unpause` refuses until that delay is guarded. The product and venue packages must therefore be published before the vault is unpaused.

Source: https://github.com/interest-protocol/blast-v2-contracts/blob/main/sui/vault/sources/blast_vault_package.move

#### Types

```move
/// The package's one-time witness (OTW), typing its version, publication, and roles.
public struct BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE() has drop;

/// The package witness `blast_admin` pins at publication: `unpause` passes it to clear pause bits.
/// Only this module constructs it, and it is never stored or returned.
public struct PackageWitness() has drop;

/// Keys the admitted witnesses' delayed lifecycle.
public struct WitnessesKey() has copy, drop, store;

/// Shared singleton holding the venue and issuer witness types that may create vaults.
public struct VaultRegistry has key {
    id: UID,
    witnesses: blast_admin::delayed_set::DelayedSet<WitnessesKey, TypeName>,
}
```

#### Functions

```move
/// Schedules `Witness`'s admission after the applied delay. Framework and primitive types are
/// rejected because anyone can construct them.
public fun schedule_witness<Witness: drop>(
    self: &mut VaultRegistry,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Applies `Witness`'s ready admission; anyone may call it.

    self: &mut VaultRegistry,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    clock: &Clock,
)

/// Cancels `Witness`'s pending admission, even a ready one.
public fun cancel_witness<Witness: drop>(
    self: &mut VaultRegistry,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::CancelAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Removes an admitted witness at once; existing vaults keep working.
public fun remove_witness<Witness: drop>(
    self: &mut VaultRegistry,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
)

/// Schedules a new admission delay after the applied one.
public fun schedule_witness_delay(
    self: &mut VaultRegistry,
    _: &blast_admin::blast_acl::AdminWitness<blast_core::blast_domain::VenueAdmin>,
    package_version: &blast_admin::package_version::PackageVersion<BLAST_VAULT_PACKAGE>,
    delay_ms: u64,
    clock: &Clock,

/// Applies a ready admission-delay change; anyone may call it.
```
